# Graph Report - codeflare  (2026-07-12)

## Corpus Check
- 1307 files · ~3,558,099 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 16063 nodes · 28992 edges · 2309 communities (658 shown, 1651 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 98% EXTRACTED · 2% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 688 edges (avg confidence: 0.7)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `57f0e202`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).

- [[_COMMUNITY_Authorization middleware separates identity, active-subscription, and admin-role checks.|Authorization middleware separates identity, active-subscription, and admin-role checks.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_Onboarding mode rewrites login to the landing login shell while SaaS keeps the SPA login.|Onboarding mode rewrites /login to the landing login shell while SaaS keeps the SPA login.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_Authentication domain covers CF Access, Direct GitHub OAuth, session cookies, JWT verification, service tokens, authorization middleware, and user provisioning.|Authentication domain covers CF Access, Direct GitHub OAuth, session cookies, JWT verification, service tokens, authorization middleware, and user provisioning.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_rclone bisync reconciles local filesystem and R2 every 15 minutes, supports manual triggers, retries failures, and falls back to resync after repeated unrecoverable failures.|rclone bisync reconciles local filesystem and R2 every 15 minutes, supports manual triggers, retries failures, and falls back to resync after repeated unrecoverable failures.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_Enterprise Browser Rendering token handling uses a non-secret container placeholder and worker-side interception scoped to the configured account's browser-rendering paths.|Enterprise Browser Rendering token handling uses a non-secret container placeholder and worker-side interception scoped to the configured account's browser-rendering paths.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_Browser Run domain defines real-browser capabilities backed by Cloudflare Browser Run for advanced-mode agents.|Browser Run domain defines real-browser capabilities backed by Cloudflare Browser Run for advanced-mode agents.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_Browser Run supports agent semantic e2e driving a deployed public app and judging acceptance criteria as a complement to scripted CI e2e.|Browser Run supports agent semantic e2e: driving a deployed public app and judging acceptance criteria as a complement to scripted CI e2e.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_Browser Run exposes both cheap one-shot REST read tools and interactive Chrome DevTools CDP control for Claude and Pi.|Browser Run exposes both cheap one-shot REST read tools and interactive Chrome DevTools CDP control for Claude and Pi.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_Non-SaaS deployments use Cloudflare Access JWTs validated against JWKS, with setup-provisioned Access appgroups.|Non-SaaS deployments use Cloudflare Access JWTs validated against JWKS, with setup-provisioned Access app/groups.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_CI monitoring is a background-agent policy coupled to PR-boundary visible handoff rather than a blocking main-session wait.|CI monitoring is a background-agent policy coupled to PR-boundary visible handoff rather than a blocking main-session wait.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_Advanced seed bundles Cloudflare platform skills for Workers, Wrangler, Agents SDK, Durable Objects, Sandbox SDK, email, Turnstile, Browser Run, and related surfaces.|Advanced seed bundles Cloudflare platform skills for Workers, Wrangler, Agents SDK, Durable Objects, Sandbox SDK, email, Turnstile, Browser Run, and related surfaces.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_Codeflare specification describes an enterprise agentic engine running autonomous coding agents in isolated Cloudflare containers with browser-native terminals and R2-backed persistence.|Codeflare specification describes an enterprise agentic engine running autonomous coding agents in isolated Cloudflare containers with browser-native terminals and R2-backed persistence.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_Global non-functional constraints cover authentication, token isolation, encryption, rate limits, security headers, performance caches, graceful shutdown, bisync recovery, circuit breakers, and idle-cost controls.|Global non-functional constraints cover authentication, token isolation, encryption, rate limits, security headers, performance caches, graceful shutdown, bisync recovery, circuit breakers, and idle-cost controls.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_Global constraints define the Cloudflare Workers, Hono, SolidJS, xterm.js, KV, R2, Containers, Durable Objects, rclone, Stripe, Resend, Vite, TypeScript, Zod, and Vitest stack.|Global constraints define the Cloudflare Workers, Hono, SolidJS, xterm.js, KV, R2, Containers, Durable Objects, rclone, Stripe, Resend, Vite, TypeScript, Zod, and Vitest stack.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_Intermediate tutorial specifies a personal CV website with Hono routes, a Turnstile-protected contact form, KV-stored messages, dark responsive design, and print CSS.|Intermediate tutorial specifies a personal CV website with Hono routes, a Turnstile-protected contact form, KV-stored messages, dark responsive design, and print CSS.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_The landing contact endpoint validates form data, verifies Turnstile, rate-limits, emails admins via Resend, escapes user fields, and persists no submission content.|The landing contact endpoint validates form data, verifies Turnstile, rate-limits, emails admins via Resend, escapes user fields, and persists no submission content.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_Deploy workflow runs install, build, tests, typecheck, Docker build, vulnerability scan, registry push, Cloudflare deploy, and secret setting.|Deploy workflow runs install, build, tests, typecheck, Docker build, vulnerability scan, registry push, Cloudflare deploy, and secret setting.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_Codeflare has mutually exclusive auth modes CF Access when GitHub OAuth is absent and Direct GitHub OAuth for SaaS mode.|Codeflare has mutually exclusive auth modes: CF Access when GitHub OAuth is absent and Direct GitHub OAuth for SaaS mode.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_E2E workflow runs setup, API, desktop UI, and mobile UI suites against a deployed worker using service-token auth.|E2E workflow runs setup, API, desktop UI, and mobile UI suites against a deployed worker using service-token auth.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_Credential values use AES-256-GCM with versioned ciphertext, per-write IV, and AAD key binding; R2 objects use SSE-C when configured.|Credential values use AES-256-GCM with versioned ciphertext, per-write IV, and AAD key binding; R2 objects use SSE-C when configured.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_Enterprise session creation restricts selectable agents to copilot, pi, and bash.|Enterprise session creation restricts selectable agents to copilot, pi, and bash.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_Enterprise Mode domain covers deploy-time single-tenant configuration, subscription bypass, and outbound HTTPS interception to customer-owned AI Gateway.|Enterprise Mode domain covers deploy-time single-tenant configuration, subscription bypass, and outbound HTTPS interception to customer-owned AI Gateway.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_ENTERPRISE_MODE forces unlimited tier, Pro session mode, and disables subscription friction.|ENTERPRISE_MODE forces unlimited tier, Pro session mode, and disables subscription friction.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_The web file browser supports R2 browsing, large multipart upload, explicit Sync-now, and traversal-safe presentation.|The web file browser supports R2 browsing, large multipart upload, explicit Sync-now, and traversal-safe presentation.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_Graceful shutdown drains final sync before stop and final-sync drain endpoint authentication is specified.|Graceful shutdown drains final sync before stop and final-sync drain endpoint authentication is specified.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_Landing first-paint stability uses dark color-schemeroot paint, immutable content-hashed assets, and view-transition rules to avoid flash during navigation.|Landing first-paint stability uses dark color-scheme/root paint, immutable content-hashed assets, and view-transition rules to avoid flash during navigation.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_Home-directory files and notes sync to Cloudflare R2 every 15 minutes plus final sync on session stop, with workspace sync discouraged by default.|Home-directory files and notes sync to Cloudflare R2 every 15 minutes plus final sync on session stop, with workspace sync discouraged by default.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_GitHub integration covers connecting accounts, repository browsing, clone actions, and per-mode token transport.|GitHub integration covers connecting accounts, repository browsing, clone actions, and per-mode token transport.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_In enterprise mode GitHub credentials are injected at the outbound egress boundary so the real token never enters the container.|In enterprise mode GitHub credentials are injected at the outbound egress boundary so the real token never enters the container.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_The GitHub panel reports connection status, lists searchablepaginated repositories, and renders connectdisconnectclone controls beside the storage panel.|The GitHub panel reports connection status, lists searchable/paginated repositories, and renders connect/disconnect/clone controls beside the storage panel.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_Connect GitHub stores a per-user token in the existing deploy-keys entry, encrypted at rest and never returned to the browser.|Connect GitHub stores a per-user token in the existing deploy-keys entry, encrypted at rest and never returned to the browser.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_Glossary provides canonical definitions for session, container, bucket, agent, session mode, preseed, tier, DO, KV, Worker, egress injection, strict egress, governed mode, and related terms.|Glossary provides canonical definitions for session, container, bucket, agent, session mode, preseed, tier, DO, KV, Worker, egress injection, strict egress, governed mode, and related terms.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_Governed Mode is an enterprise policy that disables R2 SSE-C and uses a lossless re-encrypt migration state machine to switch bucket regimes safely.|Governed Mode is an enterprise policy that disables R2 SSE-C and uses a lossless re-encrypt migration state machine to switch bucket regimes safely.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_Graphify provides knowledge-graph capability, graph-first discipline, post-clone triage, and git-persisted graph artifacts.|Graphify provides knowledge-graph capability, graph-first discipline, post-clone triage, and git-persisted graph artifacts.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_Idle sleep is based on real user input and configurable allowed values, with free-tier lock and platform idle timer inertness.|Idle sleep is based on real user input and configurable allowed values, with free-tier lock and platform idle timer inertness.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_Every session runs in its own isolated container with no shared shells or cross-session access.|Every session runs in its own isolated container with no shared shells or cross-session access.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_Landing domain defines the public enterprise marketing page, mode-aware serving, and demo-request contact pipeline.|Landing domain defines the public enterprise marketing page, mode-aware serving, and demo-request contact pipeline.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_Landing metadata includes Open Graph, Twitter Card, canonical URL, JSON-LD, robots, sitemap, llms.txt, theme color, and apple-touch-icon behavior.|Landing metadata includes Open Graph, Twitter Card, canonical URL, JSON-LD, robots, sitemap, llms.txt, theme color, and apple-touch-icon behavior.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_Enterprise LLM traffic from the container is intercepted at platform egress, mapped to the customer's AI Gateway, authenticated worker-side, and stamped with per-user attribution.|Enterprise LLM traffic from the container is intercepted at platform egress, mapped to the customer's AI Gateway, authenticated worker-side, and stamped with per-user attribution.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_Conversation context is captured every fifteen real user messages through hook-triggered transcript prefiltering, per-chunk observations, note synthesis, and vault graph merge.|Conversation context is captured every fifteen real user messages through hook-triggered transcript prefiltering, per-chunk observations, note synthesis, and vault graph merge.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_Memory domain covers Vault-based cross-session memory, automatic capture, hook delivery, graph merge, and session-mode gating.|Memory domain covers Vault-based cross-session memory, automatic capture, hook delivery, graph merge, and session-mode gating.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_Vault persistence and automatic memory machinery are Proadvanced features; Standard mode does not preserve the Vault across container recreations.|Vault persistence and automatic memory machinery are Pro/advanced features; Standard mode does not preserve the Vault across container recreations.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_Mobile Terminal domain covers touch input, virtual keyboard handling, scroll stability, and terminal rendering on mobile browsers.|Mobile Terminal domain covers touch input, virtual keyboard handling, scroll stability, and terminal rendering on mobile browsers.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_The product is optimized for phones and tablets with touch input, virtual keyboard handling, gestures, and Android scroll fixes.|The product is optimized for phones and tablets with touch input, virtual keyboard handling, gestures, and Android scroll fixes.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_Unauthenticated visitors in SaaS or onboarding mode see the prerendered landing app; default and enterprise deployments do not expose it.|Unauthenticated visitors in SaaS or onboarding mode see the prerendered landing app; default and enterprise deployments do not expose it.]]

- [[_COMMUNITY_Preseed files are generated from a Claude Code source tree and deployed as rules, skills, agents, commands, hooks, plugins, and runtime adapters.|Preseed files are generated from a Claude Code source tree and deployed as rules, skills, agents, commands, hooks, plugins, and runtime adapters.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_Advanced sessions include SDD, review, debug, deploy, brainstorm, graphify, and auto review agents.|Advanced sessions include SDD, review, debug, deploy, brainstorm, graphify, and auto review agents.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_Files persist in per-user R2 storage while containers remain ephemeral and disposable.|Files persist in per-user R2 storage while containers remain ephemeral and disposable.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_Rate limiting is per-user with persistent storage and in-memory fallback, fail-closed for security endpoints and fail-open for resource endpoints.|Rate limiting is per-user with persistent storage and in-memory fallback, fail-closed for security endpoints and fail-open for resource endpoints.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_Users can clone a selected GitHub repository into a running session or a new session workspace using the chosen branch.|Users can clone a selected GitHub repository into a running session or a new session workspace using the chosen branch.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_SaaS mode uses a branded GitHub OAuth login flow with signed stateless state, verified primary email, session cookie, and subscription-aware redirect.|SaaS mode uses a branded GitHub OAuth login flow with signed stateless state, verified primary email, session cookie, and subscription-aware redirect.]]
- [[_COMMUNITY_Containers hibernate after configurable idle timeout so cost scales with actual compute use.|Containers hibernate after configurable idle timeout so cost scales with actual compute use.]]

- [[_COMMUNITY_Agent preseed includes SDD workflows for sdd init, import mode, enrichment, source-anchor verification, clean rescue, resume mode, and enumeration coverage gates.|Agent preseed includes SDD workflows for /sdd init, import mode, enrichment, source-anchor verification, clean rescue, resume mode, and enumeration coverage gates.]]
